And He is the All-Hearer, the All-Knower (115)\nAllah tells His Prophet ﷺ to say to these polytheists who worship others besides Allah,\nأَفَغَيْرَ اللَّهِ أَبْتَغِي حَكَمًا\n(Shall I seek a judge other than Allah...) between you and I,\nوَهُوَ الَّذِي أَنزَلَ إِلَيْكُمُ الْكِتَابَ مُفَصَّلًا\n(while it is He Who has sent down unto you the Book, explained...) in detail,\nوَالَّذِينَ آتَيْنَاهُمُ الْكِتَابَ\n(and those unto whom We gave the Scripture) the Jews and the Christians,\nيَعْلَمُونَ أَنَّهُ مُنَزَّلٌ مِّن رَّبِّكَ بِالْحَقِّ\n(know that it is revealed from your Lord in truth.) because the previous Prophets have conveyed the good news of you coming to them. Allah's statement,\nفَلَا تَكُونَنَّ مِنَ الْمُمْتَرِينَ\n(So be not you of those who doubt.) is similar to His other statement,\nفَإِن كُنتَ فِي شَكٍّ مِّمَّا أَنزَلْنَا إِلَيْكَ فَاسْأَلِ الَّذِينَ يَقْرَءُونَ الْكِتَابَ مِن قَبْلِكَ لَقَدْ جَاءَكَ الْحَقُّ مِن رَّبِّكَ فَلَا تَكُونَنَّ مِنَ الْمُمْتَرِينَ\n(So if you are in doubt concerning that which We have revealed unto you, then ask those who are reading the Book before you. Verily, the truth has come to you from your Lord. So be not of those who doubt (it).)[10:94]\nThe conditional 'if' in this Ayah does not mean that 'doubt' will ever occur to the Prophet ﷺ. Allah said,\nوَتَمَّتْ كَلِمَتُ رَبِّكَ صِدْقًا وَعَدْلًا\n(And the Word of your Lord has been fulfilled in truth and in justice.)\nQatadah commented, \"In truth concerning what He stated and in justice concerning what He decided.\" Surely, whatever Allah says is the truth and He is Most Just in what He commands. All of Allah's statements are true, there is no doubt or cause for speculation about this fact, and all His commandments are pure justice, besides which there is no justice. All that He forbade is evil, for He only forbids what brings about evil consequences. Allah said in another Ayah,\nيَأْمُرُهُم بِالْمَعْرُوفِ وَيَنْهَاهُمْ عَنِ الْمُنكَرِ\n(He commands them with good; and forbids them from evil...)[7:157] until the end of the Ayah.\nلَّا مُبَدِّلَ لِكَلِمَاتِهِ\n(None can change His Words.) meaning, none can avert Allah's judgment whether in this life or the Hereafter,\nوَهُوَ السَّمِيعُ\n(And He is the All-Hearer,) Hearing, His servants' statements,\nالْعَلِيمُ\n(The All-Knower.) of their activities and lack of activity, Who awards each according to their deeds.","href":"/tafsir/6?verse=114","kind":"tafsir"},{"id":"tafsir:6:116","title":"Ibn Kathir · 6:116–117","text":"And if you obey most of those on the earth, they will mislead you far away from Allah's path. They follow nothing but conjecture, and they do nothing but lie (116)Verily, your Lord! It is He Who knows best who strays from His way, and He knows best the rightly guided (117)\n\n## Most People are Misguided\n\nAllah states that most of the people of the earth, are misguided. Allah said in other Ayat,\nوَلَقَدْ ضَلَّ قَبْلَهُمْ أَكْثَرُ الْأَوَّلِينَ\n(And indeed most of the men of old went astray before them.)[37:71] and,\nوَمَا أَكْثَرُ النَّاسِ وَلَوْ حَرَصْتَ بِمُؤْمِنِينَ\n(And most of mankind will not believe even if you eagerly desire it.)[12:103]\nThey are misguided, yet they have doubts about their way, and they rely on wishful thinking and delusions.\nإِن يَتَّبِعُونَ إِلَّا الظَّنَّ وَإِنْ هُمْ إِلَّا يَخْرُصُونَ\n(They follow nothing but conjecture, and they do nothing but lie.)\nThus, they fulfill Allah's decree and decision concerning them,\nهُوَ أَعْلَمُ مَن يَضِلُّ عَن سَبِيلِهِ\n(It is He Who knows best who strays from His way.) and facilitates that for him,\nوَهُوَ أَعْلَمُ بِالْمُهْتَدِينَ\n(And He knows best the rightly guided.) He facilitates that for them, all of them are facilitated for what He created them.","href":"/tafsir/6?verse=116","kind":"tafsir"},{"id":"tafsir:6:118","title":"Ibn Kathir · 6:118–119","text":"So eat of that on which Allah's Name has been mentioned, if you are believers in His Ayat (118)And why should you not eat of that on which Allah's Name has been mentioned, while He has explained to you in detail what is forbidden to you, except under compulsion of necessity? And surely, many do lead astray by their own desires through lack of knowledge. Certainly your Lord knows best the transgressors (119)\n\n## Allowing What was Slaughtered in the Name of Allah\n\nThis is a statement of permission from Allah, for His servants, allowing them to eat the slaughtered animals werein His Name was mentioned when slaughtering them. It is understood from it that He has not allowed that over which Allah's Name was not mentioned when slaughtering. This was the practice of the pagans of Quraysh who used to eat dead animals and eat what was slaughtered for the idols.\nAllah next encourages eating from the meat of sacrificed animals on which His Name was mentioned upon slaughtering,\nوَمَا لَكُمْ أَلَّا تَأْكُلُوا مِمَّا ذُكِرَ اسْمُ اللَّهِ عَلَيْهِ وَقَدْ فَصَّلَ لَكُم مَّا حَرَّمَ عَلَيْكُمْ\n(And why should you not eat of that on which Allah's Name has been mentioned, while He has explained to you what is forbidden to you...) meaning, He has explained and made clear to you what He has prohibited for you in detail,\nإِلَّا مَا اضْطُرِرْتُمْ إِلَيْهِ\n(except under compulsion of necessity.)\nIn which case, you are allowed to eat whatever you can find. Allah next mentions the ignorance of the idolators in their misguided ideas, such as eating dead animals and what was sacrificed while other than Allah's Name was mentioned when slaughtering them. Allah said,\nوَإِنَّ كَثِيرًا لَّيُضِلُّونَ بِأَهْوَائِهِم بِغَيْرِ عِلْمٍ إِنَّ رَبَّكَ هُوَ أَعْلَمُ بِالْمُعْتَدِينَ\n(And surely, many do lead astray by their own desires through lack of knowledge. Certainly your Lord knows best the transgressors.)\nHe has complete knowledge of their transgression, lies and inventions.","href":"/tafsir/6?verse=118","kind":"tafsir"},{"id":"tafsir:6:120","title":"Ibn Kathir · 6:120","text":"Leave sin, open and secret. Verily, those who commit sin will get due recompense for that which they used to commit.) Mujahid said tha (120)\nوَذَرُوا ظَاهِرَ الْإِثْمِ وَبَاطِنَهُ\n(Leave evil, open and secret...) refers to all kinds of sins committed in public and secret. Qatadah said that,\nوَذَرُوا ظَاهِرَ الْإِثْمِ وَبَاطِنَهُ\n(Leave sin, open and secret...) encompasses sins committed in public and secret, whether few or many. In another statement, Allah said,\nقُلْ إِنَّمَا حَرَّمَ رَبِّيَ الْفَوَاحِشَ مَا ظَهَرَ مِنْهَا وَمَا بَطَنَ\n(Say: \"(But) the things that my Lord has indeed forbidden are 'Al-Fawāḥish' (evil sins) whether committed openly or secretly.)[7:33] This is why Allah said,\nإِنَّ الَّذِينَ يَكْسِبُونَ الْإِثْمَ سَيُجْزَوْنَ بِمَا كَانُوا يَقْتَرِفُونَ\n(Verily, those who commit sin will get due recompense for that which they used to commit.)\nWhether the sins they committed were public or secret, Allah will compensate them for these sins. Ibn Abi Hatim recorded that An-Nawwas bin Sam'an said, \"I asked Allah's Messenger ﷺ about 'Al-Ithm'. He said,\nالْإِثمُ مَا حَاكَ فِي صَدْرِكَ وَكَرِهْتَ أَنْ يَطَّلِعَ النَّاسُ عَلَيْهِ\n(The sin is that which you find in your heart and you dislike that people become aware of it.)","href":"/tafsir/6?verse=120","kind":"tafsir"},{"id":"tafsir:6:121","title":"Ibn Kathir · 6:121","text":"Eat not of that on which Allah's Name has not been pronounced, for surely it is disobedience. And certainly, the Shayatin do inspire their friends to dispute with you, and if you obey them, then you would indeed be polytheists (121)\n\n## The Prohibition of what was Slaughtered in other than Allah's Name\n\nThis Ayah is used to prove that slaughtered animals are not lawful when Allah's Name is not mentioned over them – even if slaughtered by a Muslim. The Ayah about hunting game,\nفَكُلُوا مِمَّا أَمْسَكْنَ عَلَيْكُمْ وَاذْكُرُوا اسْمَ اللَّهِ عَلَيْهِ\n(So eat of what they (trained hunting dogs or birds of prey) catch for you, but pronounce the Name of Allah over it.)[5:4] supports this. The Ayah here emphasized this ruling, when Allah said,\nوَإِنَّهُ لَفِسْقٌ\n(for surely it is disobedience.)\nThey say that \"it\" refers to eating it, and others say that it refers to the sacrifice for other than Allah. There are various Hadiths that order mentioning Allah's Name when slaughtering and hunting. The Hadith narrated by 'Adi bin Hatim and Abu Tha'labah (that the Prophet ﷺ said);\nإِذَا أَرْسَلْتَ كَلْبَكَ الْمُعَلَّمَ وَذَكرتَ اسْمَ اللهِ عَلَيْهِ فَكُلْ مَا أَمْسَكَ عَلَيْكَ\n(When you send your trained hunting dog and mention Allah's Name on releasing it, then eat from whatever it catches for you.)\nThis Hadith was collected in the Two Sahihs. The Rafi' bin Khadij narrated that the Prophet ﷺ said;\nمَا أَنْهَرَ الدَّمَ وَذُكِرَ اسْمُ اللهِ عَلَيْهِ فَكُلُوهُ\n(You can use what would make blood flow (i. e., slaughter) and you can eat what is slaughtered and the Name of Allah is mentioned at the time of slaughtering.)\nThis Hadith was also collected in the Two Sahihs. Ibn Mas'ud narrated that Allah's Messenger ﷺ said to the Jinns.\nلَكُمْ كُلُّ عَظْمٍ ذُكِرَ اسْمُ اللهِ عَلَيْهِ\n((For food) you have every bone on which Allah's Name was mentioned on slaughtering.) Muslim collected this Hadith.\nJundub bin Sufyan Al-Bajali said that the Messenger of Allah ﷺ said,\nمَنْ ذَبَحَ قَبْلَ أَنْ يُصَلِّيَ فَلْيَذْبَحْ مَكَانَهَا أُخْرَى، وَمَنْ لَمْ يَكُنْ ذَبَحَ، حَتَّى صَلَّيْنَا فَلْيَذْبَحْ بِاسْمِ اللهِ\n(Whoever slaughtered before he prayed (the 'Id prayer), let him slaughter another sacrifice in its place. Whoever did not offer the sacrifice before we finished the prayer, let him slaughter and mention Allah's Name.) The Two Sahihs recorded this Hadith.\n\n## The Devil's Inspiration\n\nAllah said,\nوَإِنَّ الشَّيَاطِينَ لَيُوحُونَ إِلَىٰ أَوْلِيَائِهِمْ لِيُجَادِلُوكُمْ\n(And certainly, the Shayatin do inspire their friends to dispute with you,)\nIbn Abi Hatim recorded that Abu Ishaq said that a man said to Ibn 'Umar that Al-Mukhtar claimed that he received revelation. So Ibn 'Umar said, \"He has said the truth,\" and recited this Ayah,\nوَإِنَّ الشَّيَاطِينَ لَيُوحُونَ إِلَىٰ أَوْلِيَائِهِمْ\n(And certainly, the Shayatin do inspire their friends...)\nAbu Zamil said, \"I was sitting next to Ibn 'Abbas at a time when Al-Mukhtar bin Abi 'Ubayd was performing Hajj. So a man came to Ibn 'Abbas and said, 'O Ibn 'Abbas! Abu Ishaq (Al-Mukhtar) claimed that he received revelation this night.' Ibn 'Abbas said, He has said the truth.' I was upset and said, 'Ibn 'Abbas says that Al-Mukhtar has said the truth?' Ibn 'Abbas replied, 'There are two types of revelation, one from Allah and one from the devil. Allah's revelation came to Muhammad ﷺ, while the Shaytan's revelation comes to his friends.' He then recited,\nوَإِنَّ الشَّيَاطِينَ لَيُوحُونَ إِلَىٰ أَوْلِيَائِهِمْ\n(And certainly, the Shayatin do inspire their friends...) We also mentioned 'Ikrimah's commentary on the Ayah,\nيُوحِي بَعْضُهُمْ إِلَىٰ بَعْضٍ زُخْرُفَ الْقَوْلِ غُرُورًا\n(Inspiring one another with adorned speech as a delusion.)[6:112] Allah said next,\nلِيُجَادِلُوكُمْ\n(to dispute with you,) Ibn Jarir recorded that Ibn 'Abbas commented;\nوَلَا تَأْكُلُوا مِمَّا لَمْ يُذْكَرِ اسْمُ اللَّهِ عَلَيْهِ\n(Eat not of that on which Allah's Name has not been mentioned...) until,\nلِيُجَادِلُوكُمْ\n(...to dispute with you,) \"The devils inspire their loyal supporters, 'Do you eat from what you kill but not from what Allah causes to die?'\"\nAs-Suddi said; \"Some idolators said to the Muslims, 'You claim that you seek Allah's pleasure. Yet, you do not eat what Allah causes to die, but you eat what you slaughter?' Allah said,\nوَإِنْ أَطَعْتُمُوهُمْ\n(and if you obey them...), and eat dead animals,\nإِنَّكُمْ لَمُشْرِكُونَ\n(then you would indeed be polytheists.) Similar was said by Mujahid, Ad-Dahhak and several others among scholars of the Salaf.\n\n## Giving Preference to Anyone's Saying Over the Legislation of Allah is Shirk\n\nAllah's statement,\nوَإِنْ أَطَعْتُمُوهُمْ إِنَّكُمْ لَمُشْرِكُونَ\n(and if you obey them, then you would indeed be polytheists.) means, when you turn away from Allah's command and Legislation to the saying of anyone else, preferring other than what Allah has said, then this constitutes Shirk. Allah said in another Ayah,\nاتَّخَذُوا أَحْبَارَهُمْ وَرُهْبَانَهُمْ أَرْبَابًا مِّن دُونِ اللَّهِ\n(They (Jews and Christians) took their rabbis and their monks to be their lords besides Allah.)[9:31]\nIn explanation of this Ayah, At-Tirmidhi recorded that 'Adi bin Hatim said, \"O Allah's Messenger! They did not worship them.\" The Prophet ﷺ said,\nبَلَى إِنَّهُمْ أَحَلُّوا لَهُمُ الْحَرَامَ وَحَرَّمُوا عَلَيْهِمُ الْحَلَالَ فَاتَّبعُوهُمْ فَذَلِكَ عِبَادَتُهُمْ إِيَّاهُم\n(Yes they did. They (monks and rabbis) allowed the impermissible for them and they prohibited the lawful for them, and they followed them in that. That was their worship of them.)","href":"/tafsir/6?verse=121","kind":"tafsir"},{"id":"tafsir:6:122","title":"Ibn Kathir · 6:122","text":"Is he who was dead, and We gave him life, and set for him a light, whereby he can walk among men – like him who is in the darkness from which he can never come out? Thus it is made fair seeming to the disbelievers that which they used to do (122)\n\n## The Parable of the Disbeliever and the Believer\n\nThis is an example that Allah has given of the believer who was dead, meaning, wandering in confusion and misguidance. Then, Allah brought life to him, by bringing life to his heart with faith, guiding him to it and guiding him to obeying His Messengers,\nوَجَعَلْنَا لَهُ نُورًا يَمْشِي بِهِ فِي النَّاسِ\n(And set for him a light whereby he can walk amongst men.) for he became guided to where he should go and how to remain on the correct path. The light mentioned here is the Qur'an, according to Ibn 'Abbas, as Al-'Awfi and Ibn Abi Talhah reported from him. As-Suddi said that the light mentioned here is Islam. Both meanings are correct.\nكَمَن مَّثَلُهُ فِي الظُّلُمَاتِ\n(Like him who is in the darkness) of ignorance, desires and various types of deviation,\nلَيْسَ بِخَارِجٍ مِّنْهَا\n(From which he can never come out?) for he is unable to find a way out from what he is in. In Musnad Ahmad, it is recorded that the Prophet ﷺ said;\nإِنَّ اللهَ خَلَقَ خَلْقَهُ فِي ظُلْمَةٍ، ثُمَّ رَشَّ عَلَيْهِمْ مِنْ نُورِهِ، فَمَنْ أَصَابَهُ ذَلِكَ النُّورُ اهْتَدَى، وَمَنَ أَخْطَأَهُ ضَلَّ\n(Allah created creation in darkness, then He showered His Light upon them. Whoever was struck by that light is guided, whoever it missed is astray.) Allah said in other Ayat,\nاللَّهُ وَلِيُّ الَّذِينَ آمَنُوا يُخْرِجُهُم مِّنَ الظُّلُمَاتِ إِلَى النُّورِ وَالَّذِينَ كَفَرُوا أَوْلِيَاؤُهُمُ الطَّاغُوتُ يُخْرِجُونَهُم مِّنَ النُّورِ إِلَى الظُّلُمَاتِ أُولَٰئِكَ أَصْحَابُ النَّارِ هُمْ فِيهَا خَالِدُونَ\n(Allah is the Guardian of those who believe. He brings them out from darkness into light. But as for those who disbelieve, their friends are Taghut, they bring them out from light into darkness. Those are the dwellers of the Fire, and they will abide therein forever.)[2:257], and\nأَفَمَن يَمْشِي مُكِبًّا عَلَىٰ وَجْهِهِ أَهْدَىٰ أَمَّن يَمْشِي سَوِيًّا عَلَىٰ صِرَاطٍ مُّسْتَقِيمٍ\n(Is he who walks prone on his face, more rightly guided, or he who walks upright on the straight way?)[67:22], and\nمَثَلُ الْفَرِيقَيْنِ كَالْأَعْمَىٰ وَالْأَصَمِّ وَالْبَصِيرِ وَالسَّمِيعِ هَلْ يَسْتَوِيَانِ مَثَلًا أَفَلَا تَذَكَّرُونَ\n(The parable of the two parties is as the blind and the deaf and the seer and the hearer. Are they equal when compared? Will you not then take heed?)[11:24], and,\nوَمَا يَسْتَوِي الْأَعْمَىٰ وَالْبَصِيرُ - وَلَا الظُّلُمَاتُ وَلَا النُّورُ - وَلَا الظِّلُّ وَلَا الْحَرُورُ - وَمَا يَسْتَوِي الْأَحْيَاءُ وَلَا الْأَمْوَاتُ إِنَّ اللَّهَ يُسْمِعُ مَن يَشَاءُ وَمَا أَنتَ بِمُسْمِعٍ مَّن فِي الْقُبُورِ - إِنْ أَنتَ إِلَّا نَذِيرٌ\n(Not alike are the blind and the seeing. Nor are darkness and light. Nor are the shade and the sun's heat. Nor are the living and the dead. Verily, Allah makes whom He wills to hear, but you cannot make hear those who are in the graves. You are only a warner.)[35:19-23]\nThere are many other Ayat on this subject. We explained before why Allah mentioned the light in the singular sense and the darkness in the plural sense when we explained the Ayah at the beginning of the Surah,\nوَجَعَلَ الظُّلُمَاتِ وَالنُّورَ\n(And originated the darknesses and the light.)[6:1] Allah's statement,\nكَذَٰلِكَ زُيِّنَ لِلْكَافِرِينَ مَا كَانُوا يَعْمَلُونَ\n(Thus it is made fair seeming to the disbelievers that which they used to do.) means, We made their ignorance and misguidance appear fair to them, as Allah decreed out of His wisdom, there is no deity worthy of worship except Him alone without partners.","href":"/tafsir/6?verse=122","kind":"tafsir"},{"id":"tafsir:6:123","title":"Ibn Kathir · 6:123–124","text":"And thus We have set up in every town great ones of its wicked people to plot therein. But they plot not except against themselves, and they perceive (it) not (123)And when there comes to them a sign they say: \"We shall not believe until we receive the like of that which the Messengers of Allah received.\" Allah knows best with whom to entrust His Message. Humiliation and disgrace from Allah and a severe torment will overtake the criminals for that which they used to plot (124)\n\n## Evil Plots of the Leaders of the Criminals and their Subsequent Demise\n\nAllah says: Just as We appointed chiefs and leaders for the criminals who call to disbelief, hinder from the path of Allah, and oppose and defy you in your town, O Muhammad. Such was also the case with the Messengers before you, who were tested with the same. But the good end was always theirs.' Allah said in other Ayat,\nوَكَذَٰلِكَ جَعَلْنَا لِكُلِّ نَبِيٍّ عَدُوًّا مِّنَ الْمُجْرِمِينَ\n(Thus have We made for every Prophet an enemy among the criminals.)[25:31] Allah said,\nوَإِذَا أَرَدْنَا أَن نُّهْلِكَ قَرْيَةً أَمَرْنَا مُتْرَفِيهَا فَفَسَقُوا فِيهَا\n(And when We decide to destroy a town, We send a definite order to those among them who lead a life of luxury, and they transgress therein.)[17:16] meaning, We command them to obey Us, but they defy the command and as a consequence, We destroy them. It was also said that, \"We send a definite order\", in the last Ayah means, \"We decree for them,\" as Allah stated here\nلِيَمْكُرُوا فِيهَا\n(to plot therein.) Ibn Abi Talhah reported that Ibn 'Abbas explained the Ayah\nأَكَابِرَ مُجْرِمِيهَا لِيَمْكُرُوا فِيهَا\n(...great ones of its wicked people to plot therein.) \"We give the leadership to these wicked ones and they commit evil in it. When they do this, We destroy them with Our torment.\" Mujahid and Qatadah said that in the Ayah,\nأَكَابِرَ مُجْرِمِيهَا\n(great ones) refers to leaders. I say that this is also the meaning of Allah's statements,\nوَمَا أَرْسَلْنَا فِي قَرْيَةٍ مِّن نَّذِيرٍ إِلَّا قَالَ مُتْرَفُوهَا إِنَّا بِمَا أُرْسِلْتُم بِهِ كَافِرُونَ - وَقَالُوا نَحْنُ أَكْثَرُ أَمْوَالًا وَأَوْلَادًا وَمَا نَحْنُ بِمُعَذَّبِينَ\n(And We did not send a warner to a township, but those who were given the worldly wealth and luxuries among them, said: \"We believe not in what you have been sent with.\" And they say: \"We have too much wealth and too many children and we are not going to suffer punishment.\")[34:34-35] And,\nوَكَذَٰلِكَ مَا أَرْسَلْنَا مِن قَبْلِكَ فِي قَرْيَةٍ مِّن نَّذِيرٍ إِلَّا قَالَ مُتْرَفُوهَا إِنَّا وَجَدْنَا آبَاءَنَا عَلَىٰ أُمَّةٍ وَإِنَّا عَلَىٰ آثَارِهِم مُّقْتَدُونَ\n(And similarly, We sent not a warner before you to any town but the luxurious ones among them said: \"We found our fathers following a certain way and religion, and we will indeed follow their footsteps.\")[43:23]\nPlot' in the Ayah [6:123] refers to beautified speech and various actions with which the evil ones call to misguidance. Allah said about the people of Prophet Nuh, peace be upon him,\nوَمَكَرُوا مَكْرًا كُبَّارًا\n(And they have plotted a mighty plot.)[71:22] Allah said,\nوَلَوْ تَرَىٰ إِذِ الظَّالِمُونَ مَوْقُوفُونَ عِندَ رَبِّهِمْ يَرْجِعُ بَعْضُهُمْ إِلَىٰ بَعْضٍ الْقَوْلَ يَقُولُ الَّذِينَ اسْتُضْعِفُوا لِلَّذِينَ اسْتَكْبَرُوا لَوْلَا أَنتُمْ لَكُنَّا مُؤْمِنِينَ - قَالَ الَّذِينَ اسْتَكْبَرُوا لِلَّذِينَ اسْتُضْعِفُوا أَنَحْنُ صَدَدْنَاكُمْ عَنِ الْهُدَىٰ بَعْدَ إِذْ جَاءَكُم بَلْ كُنتُم مُّجْرِمِينَ - وَقَالَ الَّذِينَ اسْتُضْعِفُوا لِلَّذِينَ اسْتَكْبَرُوا بَلْ مَكْرُ اللَّيْلِ وَالنَّهَارِ إِذْ تَأْمُرُونَنَا أَن نَّكْفُرَ بِاللَّهِ وَنَجْعَلَ لَهُ أَندَادًا\n(But if you could see when the wrongdoers will be made to stand before their Lord, how they will cast the (blaming) word one to another! Those who were deemed weak will say to those who were arrogant: \"Had it not been for you, we should certainly have been believers.\" And those who were arrogant will say to those who were deemed weak: \"Did we keep you back from guidance after it had come to you? Nay, but you were criminals.\" Those who were deemed weak will say to those who were arrogant: \"Nay, but it was your plotting by night and day, when you ordered us to disbelieve in Allah and set up rivals for Him!\")[34:31-33]. Ibn Abi Hatim reported that Ibn Abi 'Umar said that Sufyan said, \"Every 'plot' mentioned in the Qur'an refers to actions.\" Allah's statement,\nوَمَا يَمْكُرُونَ إِلَّا بِأَنفُسِهِمْ وَمَا يَشْعُرُونَ\n(But they plot not except against themselves, and they perceive (it) not.) means, the harm of their wicked plots, as well as misguiding those whom they lead astray, will only strike them. Allah said in other Ayat,\nوَلَيَحْمِلُنَّ أَثْقَالَهُمْ وَأَثْقَالًا مَّعَ أَثْقَالِهِمْ\n(And verily, they shall bear their own loads, and other loads besides their own.)[29:13] and,\nوَمِنْ أَوْزَارِ الَّذِينَ يُضِلُّونَهُم بِغَيْرِ عِلْمٍ أَلَا سَاءَ مَا يَزِرُونَ\n(And also of the burdens of those whom they misled without knowledge. Evil indeed is that which they shall bear!)[16:25]. Allah said;\nوَإِذَا جَاءَتْهُمْ آيَةٌ قَالُوا لَن نُّؤْمِنَ حَتَّىٰ نُؤْتَىٰ مِثْلَ مَا أُوتِيَ رُسُلُ اللَّهِ\n(And when there comes to them a sign they say: \"We shall not believe until we receive the like of that which the Messengers of Allah received.\") When there comes to them a sign they say,\nلَن نُّؤْمِنَ حَتَّىٰ نُؤْتَىٰ مِثْلَ مَا أُوتِيَ رُسُلُ اللَّهِ\n(\"We shall not believe until we receive the like of that which the Messengers of Allah received.\") until the angels bring us the Message from Allah, just as they brought it to the Messengers. In another Ayah, Allah said,\nوَقَالَ الَّذِينَ لَا يَرْجُونَ لِقَاءَنَا لَوْلَا أُنزِلَ عَلَيْنَا الْمَلَائِكَةُ أَوْ نَرَىٰ رَبَّنَا\n(And those who expect not a meeting with Us said: \"Why are not the angels sent down to us, or why do we not see our Lord?\")[25:21]. Allah's statement,\nاللَّهُ أَعْلَمُ حَيْثُ يَجْعَلُ رِسَالَتَهُ\n(Allah knows best with whom to entrust His Message.) means, He knows best with whom His Message should be given and which of His creatures are suitable for it. Allah said in other Ayat,\nوَقَالُوا لَوْلَا نُزِّلَ هَٰذَا الْقُرْآنُ عَلَىٰ رَجُلٍ مِّنَ الْقَرْيَتَيْنِ عَظِيمٍ - أَهُمْ يَقْسِمُونَ رَحْمَتَ رَبِّكَ\n(And they say: \"Why is not this Qur'an sent down to some great man of the two towns?\" Is it they who would portion out the mercy of your Lord?)[43:31-32].\nThey said, why was not this Qur'an revealed to a mighty, respectable leader, honored by us,\nمِّنَ الْقَرْيَتَيْنِ\n(...from one of the two towns) Of Makkah and At-Ta'if. This is because they, may Allah curse them, belittled the Messenger ﷺ out of envy, transgression, rebellion and defiance. Allah described them,\nوَإِذَا رَأَوْكَ إِن يَتَّخِذُونَكَ إِلَّا هُزُوًا أَهَٰذَا الَّذِي بَعَثَ اللَّهُ رَسُولًا\n(And when they see you, they only mock: \"Is this the one whom Allah has sent as a Messenger?\")[25:41] and\nوَإِذَا رَآكَ الَّذِينَ كَفَرُوا إِن يَتَّخِذُونَكَ إِلَّا هُزُوًا أَهَٰذَا الَّذِي يَذْكُرُ آلِهَتَكُمْ وَهُم بِذِكْرِ الرَّحْمَٰنِ هُمْ كَافِرُونَ\n(And when those who disbelieved see you, they only mock at you: \"Is this the one who talks about your gods?\" While they disbelieve at the mention of the Most Gracious (Allah).)[21:36], and,\nوَلَقَدِ اسْتُهْزِئَ بِرُسُلٍ مِّن قَبْلِكَ فَحَاقَ بِالَّذِينَ سَخِرُوا مِنْهُم مَّا كَانُوا بِهِ يَسْتَهْزِئُونَ\n(Indeed Messengers were mocked before, but the scoffers were surrounded by that, whereat they used to mock.)[21:41]\n\n## The Disbelievers Admit to the Prophet's Nobility of Lineage\n\nThe disbelievers did all of this although they admitted to the Prophet's virtue, honorable lineage, respectable ancestry and purity of household and upbringing, may Allah, His angels, and the believers send blessings upon him. The disbelievers used to call the Prophet ﷺ, before he received revelation, 'Al-Amin' – the Truthful. The leader of the Quraysh disbelievers, Abu Sufyan, had to admit to this fact when Heraclius, emperor of Rome, asked him, \"How honorable is his (the Prophet's) ancestral lineage among you?\" Abu Sufyan answered, \"His ancestry is highly regarded among us.\" Heraclius asked, \"Do you find that he lied, before he started his mission?\" Abu Sufyan replied, \"No.\" The emperor of Rome relied on the honor and purity of the Prophet ﷺ to recognize the truth of his prophethood and what he came with.\nImam Ahmad recorded that Wathilah bin Al-Asqa' said that the Messenger of Allah ﷺ said,\nإِنَّ اللهَ اصْطَفَى مِنْ وَلَدِ إِبْرَاهِيمَ إِسْمَاعِيلَ، وَاصْطَفَى مِنْ بَنِي إِسْمَاعِيلَ بَنِي كِنَانَةَ وَاصْطَفَى مِنْ بَنِي كِنَانَةَ قُرَيْشًا وَاصْطَفَى مِنْ قرَيْشٍ بَنِي هَاشِمٍ وَاصْطَفَانِي مِنْ بَنِي هَاشِمٍ\n(Verily, Allah has chosen Isma'il from the offspring of Ibrahim, Bani Kinanah from the offspring of Isma'il, Quraysh from Bani Kinanah, Bani Hashim from Quraysh and, He has chosen me from Bani Hashim.) Muslim recorded this Hadith.\nAl-Bukhari recorded that Abu Hurayrah said that the Messenger of Allah ﷺ said,\nبُعِثْتُ مِنْ خَيْرِ قُرُونِ بَنِي آدَمَ قَرْنًا فَقَرْنًا، حَتَّى بُعِثْتُ مِنَ الْقَرْنِ الَّذِي كُنْتُ فِيهِ\n(I was chosen from a succession of the best generations of the Children of Adam, until the generation I was sent in.) Allah's said,\nسَيُصِيبُ الَّذِينَ أَجْرَمُوا صَغَارٌ عِندَ اللَّهِ وَعَذَابٌ شَدِيدٌ\n(Humiliation and disgrace from Allah and a severe torment will overtake the criminals...)\nThis is a stern threat and sure promise from Allah for those who arrogantly refrain from obeying His Messengers and adhering to what they came with. On the Day of Resurrection, they will suffer humiliation and eternal disgrace before Allah, because they were arrogant in the worldly life. This is why it is befitting that they earn disgrace on the Day of Resurrection. Allah said in another Ayah,\nإِنَّ الَّذِينَ يَسْتَكْبِرُونَ عَنْ عِبَادَتِي سَيَدْخُلُونَ جَهَنَّمَ دَاخِرِينَ\n(Verily, those who scorn My worship, they will surely enter Hell in humiliation!)[40:60] disgrace and dishonor. Allah said next,\nوَعَذَابٌ شَدِيدٌ بِمَا كَانُوا يَمْكُرُونَ\n(and a severe torment for that which they used to plot.)\nSince plotting usually takes place in secret and involves treachery and deceit, the disbelievers were recompensed with severe torment from Allah on the Day of Resurrection, as a just reckoning,\nوَلَا يَظْلِمُ رَبُّكَ أَحَدًا\n(And your Lord treats no one with injustice)[18:49] Allah said in another Ayah,\nيَوْمَ تُبْلَى السَّرَآئِرُ\n(The Day when all the secrets will be examined.)[86:9] Meaning, the secrets, hidden thoughts and intentions will be exposed.\nIn the Two Sahihs, it is recorded that the Messenger of Allah ﷺ said,\nيُنْصَبُ لِكُلِّ غَادِرٍ لِوَاءٌ عِنْدَ اسْتِهِ يَوْمَ الْقِيَامَةِ، فَيُقَالُ: هَذِهِ غَدْرَةُ فُلَانِ بْنِ فُلَانِ بْنِ فُلَانٍ\n(A banner will be raised for every deceitful person from his anus on the Day of Resurrection, and it will say; 'This is the treacherous plot of so-and-so, son of so-and-so, son of so-and-so.,)\nThe wisdom in this is that since a plot occurs in secret, and people are usually unaware of it, then on the Day of Resurrection the plot itself will become public news testifying to the actions of those who committed it.","href":"/tafsir/6?verse=123","kind":"tafsir"},{"id":"tafsir:6:125","title":"Ibn Kathir · 6:125","text":"And whomsoever Allah wills to guide, He opens his breast to Islam; and whomsoever He wills to send astray, He makes his breast closed and constricted, as if he is climbing up to the sky. Thus Allah puts the wrath on those who believe not.) Allah sai (125)\nفَمَن يُرِدِ اللَّهُ أَن يَهْدِيَهُ يَشْرَحْ صَدْرَهُ لِلْإِسْلَامِ\n(And whomsoever Allah wills to guide, He opens his breast to Islam;)\nHe makes Islam easy for him and strengthens his resolve to embrace it, and these are good signs. Allah said in other Ayat,\nأَفَمَن شَرَحَ اللَّهُ صَدْرَهُ لِلْإِسْلَامِ فَهُوَ عَلَىٰ نُورٍ مِّن رَّبِّهِ\n(Is he whose breast Allah has opened to Islam, so that he is in light from His Lord (as he who is a non-Muslim)?)[39:22] and,\nوَلَٰكِنَّ اللَّهَ حَبَّبَ إِلَيْكُمُ الْإِيمَانَ وَزَيَّنَهُ فِي قُلُوبِكُمْ وَكَرَّهَ إِلَيْكُمُ الْكُفْرَ وَالْفُسُوقَ وَالْعِصْيَانَ أُولَٰئِكَ هُمُ الرَّاشِدُونَ\n(But Allah has endeared the faith to you and has beautified it in your hearts, and has made disbelief, wickedness and disobedience hated by you. Such are they who are the rightly guided.)[49:7]\nIbn 'Abbas commented on Allah's statement,\nفَمَن يُرِدِ اللَّهُ أَن يَهْدِيَهُ يَشْرَحْ صَدْرَهُ لِلْإِسْلَامِ\n(And whomsoever Allah wills to guide, He opens his breast to Islam;),\n\"Allah says that He will open his heart to Tawhid and faith in Him.\" This is the same as was reported from Abu Malik and several others, and it is sound. Allah's statement,\nوَمَن يُرِدْ أَن يُضِلَّهُ يَجْعَلْ صَدْرَهُ ضَيِّقًا حَرَجًا\n(and whomsoever He wills to send astray, He makes his breast closed and constricted,) refers to inability to accept guidance, thus being deprived of beneficial faith.\nكَأَنَّمَا يَصَّعَّدُ فِي السَّمَاءِ\n(...as if he is climbing up to the sky.) because of the heaviness of faith on him. Sa'id bin Jubayr commented that in this case, \"(Islam) finds every path in his heart impassable.\"\nAl-Hakam bin Aban said that 'Ikrimah narrated from Ibn 'Abbas that he commented on:\nكَأَنَّمَا يَصَّعَّدُ فِي السَّمَاءِ\n(...as if he is climbing up to the sky),\n\"Just as the Son of Adam cannot climb up to the sky, Tawhid and faith will not be able to enter his heart, until Allah decides to allow it into his heart.\"\nImam Abu Ja'far bin Jarir commented: \"This is a parable that Allah has given for the heart of the disbeliever, which is completely impassable and closed to faith. Allah says, the example of the disbeliever's inability to accept faith in his heart and that it is too small to accommodate it, is the example of his inability to climb up to the sky, which is beyond his capability and power.\" He also commented on Allah's statement,\nكَذَٰلِكَ يَجْعَلُ اللَّهُ الرِّجْسَ عَلَى الَّذِينَ لَا يُؤْمِنُونَ\n(Thus Allah puts the Rijs (wrath) on those who believe not.)\n\"Allah says that just as He makes the heart of whomever He decides to misguide, closed and constricted, He also appoints Shaytan for him and for his likes, those who refused to believe in Allah and His Messenger. Consequently, Shaytan lures and hinders them from the path of Allah.\" 'Ali bin Abi Talhah reported that Ibn 'Abbas said that, Rijs, refers to Shaytan, while Mujahid said that it refers to all that does not contain goodness. 'Abdur-Rahman bin Zayd bin Aslam said that, Rijs, means, 'torment'.","href":"/tafsir/6?verse=125","kind":"tafsir"},{"id":"tafsir:6:126","title":"Ibn Kathir · 6:126–127","text":"And this is the path of your Lord leading straight. We have detailed Our Ayat for a people who take heed (126)For them will be the abode of peace with their Lord. And He will be their Wali because of what they used to do (127)\nAfter Allah mentioned the way of those who were themselves led to stray from His path and who hindered others from it, He emphasized the honor of the guidance and religion of truth that He sent His Messenger with. Allah said next,\nوَهَٰذَا صِرَاطُ رَبِّكَ مُسْتَقِيمًا\n(And this is the path of your Lord leading straight.) that is, Islam, that We have legislated for you, O Muhammad, by revealing this Qur'an to you, is Allah's straight path.\nقَدْ فَصَّلْنَا الْآيَاتِ\n(We have detailed Our Ayat...) We have explained the Ayat and made them clear and plain,\nلِقَوْمٍ يَذَّكَّرُونَ\n(for a people who take heed) those who have sound comprehension and understand what Allah and His Messenger convey to them,\nلَهُمْ دَارُ السَّلَامِ\n(For them will be the abode of peace) Paradise,\nعِندَ رَبِّهِمْ\n(with their Lord.) on the Day of Resurrection. Allah described Paradise as 'the abode of peace', because its residents are safe due to their access to the straight path, which conforms to the way of the Prophets. And just as their way was not wicked, they earned the abode of peace [which is free from all wickedness].\nوَهُوَ وَلِيُّهُم\n(And He will be their Wali) Protector, Supporter and Helper,\nبِمَا كَانُوا يَعْمَلُونَ\n(because of what they used to do,) As reward for their good deeds, Allah has favored them and been generous with them, and awarded them Paradise.","href":"/tafsir/6?verse=126","kind":"tafsir"},{"id":"tafsir:6:128","title":"Ibn Kathir · 6:128","text":"And on the Day when He will gather them together (and say): \"O you assembly of Jinn! Many did you mislead of men,\" and their friends among the people will say: \"Our Lord! We benefited one from the other, but now we have reached our appointed term which You did appoint for us. \" He will say: \"The Fire be your dwelling place, you will dwell therein forever, except as Allah may will. Certainly your Lord is All-Wise, All-Knowing. (128)\nAllah says, 'Mention, O Muhammad, in what you convey and warn,' that,\nوَيَوْمَ يَحْشُرُهُمْ جَمِيعًا\n(on the Day when He will gather them (all) together.) gather the Jinns and their loyal supporters from mankind who used to worship them in this life, seek refuge with them, obey them and inspire each other with adorned, deceitful speech. Allah will proclaim then,\nيَا مَعْشَرَ الْجِنِّ قَدِ اسْتَكْثَرْتُم مِّنَ الْإِنسِ\n(O you assembly of Jinn! Many did you mislead of men,) So the Ayah;\nقَدِ اسْتَكْثَرْتُم مِّنَ الْإِنسِ\n(Many did you mislead of men) refers to their misguiding and leading them astray. Allah also said;\nأَلَمْ أَعْهَدْ إِلَيْكُمْ يَا بَنِي آدَمَ أَن لَّا تَعْبُدُوا الشَّيْطَانَ إِنَّهُ لَكُمْ عَدُوٌّ مُّبِينٌ - وَأَنِ اعْبُدُونِي هَٰذَا صِرَاطٌ مُّسْتَقِيمٌ - وَلَقَدْ أَضَلَّ مِنكُمْ جِبِلًّا كَثِيرًا أَفَلَمْ تَكُونُوا تَعْقِلُونَ\n(Did I not command you, O Children of Adam, that you should not worship Shaytan. Verily, he is a plain enemy to you. And that you should worship Me. That is the straight path. And indeed he (Shaytan) did lead astray a great multitude of you. Did you not, then, understand?)[36:60-62], and\nوَقَالَ أَوْلِيَاؤُهُم مِّنَ الْإِنسِ رَبَّنَا اسْتَمْتَعَ بَعْضُنَا بِبَعْضٍ\n(and their friends among the people will say: \"Our Lord! We benefited one from the other...\")\nThe friends of the Jinns among humanity will give this answer to Allah, after Allah chastises them for being misguided by the Jinns. Al-Hasan commented, \"They benefited from each other when the Jinns merely commanded and mankind obeyed.\"\nIbn Jurayj said, \"During the time of Jahiliyyah, a man would reach a land and proclaim, 'I seek refuge with the master (Jinn) of this valley,' and this is how they benefited from each other. They used this as an excuse for them on the Day of Resurrection.\" Therefore, the Jinns benefit from humans since humans revere the Jinns by invoking them for help. The Jinns would then proclaim, \"We became the masters of both mankind and the Jinns.\"\nوَبَلَغْنَا أَجَلَنَا الَّذِي أَجَّلْتَ لَنَا\n(but now we have reached our appointed term which You did appoint for us.) meaning, death, according to As-Suddi.\nقَالَ النَّارُ مَثْوَاكُمْ\n(He (Allah) will say: \"The Fire be your dwelling place...\") where you will reside and live, you and your friends,\nخَالِدِينَ فِيهَا\n(you will dwell therein forever.) and will never depart except what Allah may will.","href":"/tafsir/6?verse=128","kind":"tafsir"},{"id":"tafsir:6:129","title":"Ibn Kathir · 6:129","text":"And thus We do make the wrongdoers supporters of one another, because of that which they used to earn (129)\n\n## The Wrongdoers Are the Supporters of Each other\n\nMa'mar said that Qatadah commented on this Ayah, \"Allah makes the wrongdoers supporters for each other in the Fire by following one another into it.\" 'Abdur-Rahman bin Zayd bin Aslam commented on Allah's statement,\nوَكَذَٰلِكَ نُوَلِّي بَعْضَ الظَّالِمِينَ بَعْضًا\n(And thus We do make the wrongdoers supporters of one another.)\n\"It refers to the wrongdoers of the Jinns and mankind.\" He then recited,\nوَمَن يَعْشُ عَن ذِكْرِ الرَّحْمَٰنِ نُقَيِّضْ لَهُ شَيْطَانًا فَهُوَ لَهُ قَرِينٌ\n(And whosoever turns away blindly from the remembrance of the Most Gracious (Allah), We appoint for him Shaytan to be a companion to him.)[43:36]\nHe said next – concerning the meaning of the Ayah; \"We appoint the wrongdoer of the Jinns over the wrongdoer of mankind.\" A poet once said, \"There is no hand, but Allah's Hand is above it, and no wrongdoer but will be tested by another wrongdoer.\"\nThe meaning of this honorable Ayah thus becomes: 'Just as We made this losing group of mankind supporters of the Jinns that misguided them, We also appoint the wrongdoers over one another, destroy them by the hands of one another, and take revenge from them with one another. This is the just recompense for their injustice and transgression.'","href":"/tafsir/6?verse=129","kind":"tafsir"},{"id":"tafsir:6:130","title":"Ibn Kathir · 6:130","text":"O you assembly of Jinn and humans! \"Did not there come to you Messengers from amongst you, reciting unto you My verses and warning you of the meeting of this Day of yours?\" They will say: \"We bear witness against ourselves.\" It was the life of this world that deceived them. And they will bear witness against themselves that they were disbelievers (130)\n\n## Chastising the Jinns and Humans after their Admission that Allah Sent Messengers to Them\n\nAllah will chastise the disbelieving Jinns and humans on the Day of Resurrection, when He asks them, while having better knowledge, if the Messengers delivered His Messages to them,\nيَا مَعْشَرَ الْجِنِّ وَالْإِنسِ أَلَمْ يَأْتِكُمْ رُسُلٌ مِّنكُمْ\n(\"O you assembly of Jinn and humans! Did not there come to you Messengers from among you?\")\nWe should note here that the Messengers are from among mankind only, not vice versa, as Mujahid, Ibn Jurayj and others from the Imams of Salaf and later generations have stated. The proof for this is that Allah said,\nإِنَّا أَوْحَيْنَا إِلَيْكَ كَمَا أَوْحَيْنَا إِلَىٰ نُوحٍ وَالنَّبِيِّينَ مِن بَعْدِهِ\n(Verily, We have sent the revelation to you as We sent the revelation to Nuh and the Prophets after him.)[4:163], until,\nرُّسُلًا مُّبَشِّرِينَ وَمُنذِرِينَ لِئَلَّا يَكُونَ لِلنَّاسِ عَلَى اللَّهِ حُجَّةٌ بَعْدَ الرُّسُلِ\n(Messengers as bearers of good news as well as of warning in order that mankind should have no plea against Allah after the (coming of) Messengers.)[4:165] Allah said, concerning the Prophet Ibrahim,\nوَجَعَلْنَا فِي ذُرِّيَّتِهِ النُّبُوَّةَ وَالْكِتَابَ\n(And We ordained among his offspring prophethood and the Book)[29:27], thus sending the prophethood and the Book exclusively through the offspring of the Prophet Ibrahim. No one has claimed that there were Prophets from among the Jinns before the time of Ibrahim, but not after that. Allah said,\nوَمَا أَرْسَلْنَا قَبْلَكَ مِنَ الْمُرْسَلِينَ إِلَّا إِنَّهُمْ لَيَأْكُلُونَ الطَّعَامَ وَيَمْشُونَ فِي الْأَسْوَاقِ\n(And We never sent before you any of the Messengers but verily, they ate food and walked in the markets.)[25:20], and,\nوَمَا أَرْسَلْنَا مِن قَبْلِكَ إِلَّا رِجَالًا نُّوحِي إِلَيْهِم مِّنْ أَهْلِ الْقُرَىٰ\n(And We sent not before you any but men unto whom We revealed, from among the people of townships.)[12:109]\nTherefore, concerning prophethood, the Jinns follow mankind in this regard and this is why Allah said about them,\nوَإِذْ صَرَفْنَا إِلَيْكَ نَفَرًا مِّنَ الْجِنِّ يَسْتَمِعُونَ الْقُرْآنَ فَلَمَّا حَضَرُوهُ قَالُوا أَنصِتُوا فَلَمَّا قُضِيَ وَلَّوْا إِلَىٰ قَوْمِهِم مُّنذِرِينَ - قَالُوا يَا قَوْمَنَا إِنَّا سَمِعْنَا كِتَابًا أُنزِلَ مِن بَعْدِ مُوسَىٰ مُصَدِّقًا لِّمَا بَيْنَ يَدَيْهِ يَهْدِي إِلَى الْحَقِّ وَإِلَىٰ طَرِيقٍ مُّسْتَقِيمٍ - يَا قَوْمَنَا أَجِيبُوا دَاعِيَ اللَّهِ وَآمِنُوا بِهِ يَغْفِرْ لَكُم مِّن ذُنُوبِكُمْ وَيُجِرْكُم مِّنْ عَذَابٍ أَلِيمٍ - وَمَن لَّا يُجِبْ دَاعِيَ اللَّهِ فَلَيْسَ بِمُعْجِزٍ فِي الْأَرْضِ وَلَيْسَ لَهُ مِن دُونِهِ أَوْلِيَاءُ أُولَٰئِكَ فِي ضَلَالٍ مُّبِينٍ\n(And (remember) when We sent towards you a group of the Jinn, listening to the Qur'an. When they stood in the presence thereof, they said: \"Listen in silence!\" And when it was finished, they returned to their people, as warners. They said: \"O our people! Verily, we have heard a Book sent down after Musa, confirming what came before it, it guides to the truth and to the straight way. O our people! Respond to Allah's caller, and believe in him. He (Allah) will forgive you your sins, and will save you from a painful torment (i.e. Hell-fire). And whosoever does not respond to Allah's caller, he cannot escape on earth, and there will be no helpers for him besides Allah. Those are in manifest error.)[46:29-32]\nA Hadith collected by At-Tirmidhi stated that the Messenger of Allah ﷺ recited Surat Ar-Rahman, to these Jinns, in which Allah said,\nسَنَفْرُغُ لَكُمْ أَيُّهَ الثَّقَلَانِ - فَبِأَيِّ آلَاءِ رَبِّكُمَا تُكَذِّبَانِ\n(We shall attend to you, O you two classes (Jinn and men)! Then which of the blessings of your Lord will you both (Jinn and men) deny)[55:31-32] Allah said in this honorable Ayah,\nيَا مَعْشَرَ الْجِنِّ وَالْإِنسِ أَلَمْ يَأْتِكُمْ رُسُلٌ مِّنكُمْ يَقُصُّونَ عَلَيْكُمْ آيَاتِي وَيُنذِرُونَكُمْ لِقَاءَ يَوْمِكُمْ هَٰذَا قَالُوا شَهِدْنَا عَلَىٰ أَنفُسِنَا\n(O you assembly of Jinn and humans! \"Did not there come to you Messengers from amongst you, reciting unto you My verses and warning you of the meeting of this Day of yours?\" They will say: \"We bear witness against ourselves.\") meaning, we affirm that the Messengers have conveyed Your Messages to us and warned us about the meeting with You, and that this Day will certainly occur. Allah said next,\nوَغَرَّتْهُمُ الْحَيَاةُ الدُّنْيَا\n(It was the life of this world that deceived them.) and they wasted their lives and brought destruction to themselves by rejecting the Messengers and denying their miracles. This is because they were deceived by the beauty, adornment and lusts of this life.\nوَشَهِدُوا عَلَىٰ أَنفُسِهِمْ\n(And they will bear witness against themselves) on the Day of Resurrection,\nأَنَّهُمْ كَانُوا كَافِرِينَ\n(that they were disbelievers...) in this worldly life, rejecting what the Messengers, may Allah's peace and blessings be on them, brought them.","href":"/tafsir/6?verse=130","kind":"tafsir"},{"id":"tafsir:6:131","title":"Ibn Kathir · 6:131–132","text":"This is because your Lord would not destroy the (populations of) towns for their wrongdoing while their people were unaware (131)For all there will be degrees according to what they did. And your Lord is not unaware of what they do.) Allah sai (132)\nذَٰلِكَ أَن لَّمْ يَكُن رَّبُّكَ مُهْلِكَ الْقُرَىٰ بِظُلْمٍ وَأَهْلُهَا غَافِلُونَ\n(This is because your Lord would not destroy the (populations of) towns for their wrongdoing while their people were unaware.) meaning: 'We sent the Messengers and revealed the Books to the Jinns and mankind, so that no one has an excuse that he is being punished for his wrongs although he did not receive Allah's Message. Therefore, We did not punish any of the nations, except after sending Messengers to them, so that they have no excuse.' Allah said in other Ayat,\nوَإِن مِّنْ أُمَّةٍ إِلَّا خَلَا فِيهَا نَذِيرٌ\n(And there never was a nation but a warner had passed among them.)[35:24], and\nوَلَقَدْ بَعَثْنَا فِي كُلِّ أُمَّةٍ رَّسُولًا أَنِ اعْبُدُوا اللَّهَ وَاجْتَنِبُوا الطَّاغُوتَ\n(And verily, We have sent among every Ummah a Messenger (proclaiming): \"Worship Allah, and stay away from At-Taghut (all false deities).\")[16:36], and\nوَمَا كُنَّا مُعَذِّبِينَ حَتَّىٰ نَبْعَثَ رَسُولًا\n(And We never punish until We have sent a Messenger.)[17:15], and,\nكُلَّمَا أُلْقِيَ فِيهَا فَوْجٌ سَأَلَهُمْ خَزَنَتُهَا أَلَمْ يَأْتِكُمْ نَذِيرٌ - قَالُوا بَلَىٰ قَدْ جَاءَنَا نَذِيرٌ فَكَذَّبْنَا\n(Every time a group is cast therein, its keeper will ask: \"Did no warner come to you?\" They will say: \"Yes, indeed a warner did come to us, but we belied him.\")[67:8-9]\nThere are many other Ayat on this subject. At-Tabari said, \"Allah's statement,\nوَلِكُلٍّ دَرَجَاتٌ مِّمَّا عَمِلُوا\n(For all there will be degrees according to what they did.) means, every person who obeys Allah or behaves disobediently, has grades and ranks according to their works, which Allah gives them as recompense, good for good and evil for evil.\" I say, it is possible that Allah's statement,\nوَلِكُلٍّ دَرَجَاتٌ مِّمَّا عَمِلُوا\n(For all there will be degrees according to what they did.) refers to the disbelievers of the Jinns and mankind who will earn a place in the Fire according to their evil deeds. Allah said,\nقَالَ لِكُلٍّ ضِعْفٌ\n(He will say: \"For each one there is double (torment).\")[7:38], and,\nالَّذِينَ كَفَرُوا وَصَدُّوا عَن سَبِيلِ اللَّهِ زِدْنَاهُمْ عَذَابًا فَوْقَ الْعَذَابِ بِمَا كَانُوا يُفْسِدُونَ\n(Those who disbelieved and hinder (others) from the path of Allah, for them We will add torment to the torment because they used to spread corruption.)[16:88] Allah said next,\nوَمَا رَبُّكَ بِغَافِلٍ عَمَّا يَعْمَلُونَ\n(And your Lord is not unaware of what they do.)\nIbn Jarir commented, \"All these deeds that they did, O Muhammad, they did while your Lord is aware of them, and He collects and records these deeds with Him, so that He recompenses them when they meet Him and return to Him.","href":"/tafsir/6?verse=131","kind":"tafsir"},{"id":"tafsir:6:133","title":"Ibn Kathir · 6:133–135","text":"And your Lord is Al-Ghani, full of mercy; if He wills, He can destroy you; and in your place make whom He wills as your successors, as He raised you from the seed of other people (133)Surely, that which you are promised, will verily come to pass, and you cannot escape (134)Say: \"O my people! Work according to your way, surely, I too am working, and you will come to know for which of us will be the (happy) end in the Hereafter. Certainly the wrongdoers will not be successful. (135)\n\n## If They Disobey, They Will Perish\n\nAllah said,\nوَرَبُّكَ\n(And your Lord...), O Muhammad,\nالْغَنِيُّ\n(is 'Al-Ghani') Rich, free from needing His creatures in any way or form, while they stand in need of Him in all situations,\nذُو الرَّحْمَةِ\n(full of mercy;) towards creation. Allah said in another Ayah,\nإِنَّ اللَّهَ بِالنَّاسِ لَرَءُوفٌ رَّحِيمٌ\n(Truly, Allah is full of kindness, the Most Merciful towards mankind.)[2:143]\nإِن يَشَأْ يُذْهِبْكُمْ\n(if He wills, He can destroy you.) if you defy His commandments,\nوَيَسْتَخْلِفْ مِن بَعْدِكُم مَّا يَشَاءُ\n(And in your place make whom He wills as your successors,) who behave obediently,\nكَمَا أَنشَأَكُم مِّن ذُرِّيَّةِ قَوْمٍ آخَرِينَ\n(As He raised you from the seed of other people.) and surely, He is able to do this, and it is easy for Him. And just as Allah has destroyed the earlier nations and brought their successors, He is able to do away with these generations and bring other people in their place. Allah has also said;\nإِن يَشَأْ يُذْهِبْكُمْ أَيُّهَا النَّاسُ وَيَأْتِ بِآخَرِينَ وَكَانَ اللَّهُ عَلَىٰ ذَٰلِكَ قَدِيرًا\n(If He wills, He can take you away, O people, and bring others. And Allah is Ever Capable over that.)[4:133],\nيَا أَيُّهَا النَّاسُ أَنتُمُ الْفُقَرَاءُ إِلَى اللَّهِ وَاللَّهُ هُوَ الْغَنِيُّ الْحَمِيدُ - إِن يَشَأْ يُذْهِبْكُمْ وَيَأْتِ بِخَلْقٍ جَدِيدٍ - وَمَا ذَٰلِكَ عَلَى اللَّهِ بِعَزِيزٍ\n(O mankind! It is you who stand in need of Allah. But Allah is Rich (free of all needs), Worthy of all praise. If He willed, He could destroy you and bring about a new creation. And that is not hard for Allah.)[35:15-17], and,\nوَاللَّهُ الْغَنِيُّ وَأَنتُمُ الْفُقَرَاءُ وَإِن تَتَوَلَّوْا يَسْتَبْدِلْ قَوْمًا غَيْرَكُمْ ثُمَّ لَا يَكُونُوا أَمْثَالَكُم\n(But Allah is Rich (free of all needs), and you are poor. And if you turn away, He will exchange you for some other people and they will not be your likes.)[47:38].\nMuhammad bin Ishaq said that Ya'qub bin 'Utbah said that he heard Aban bin 'Uthman saying about this Ayah,\nكَمَا أَنشَأَكُم مِّن ذُرِّيَّةِ قَوْمٍ آخَرِينَ\n(As He raised you from the seed of other people.) \"The seed means the offspring and the children.\" Allah's statement,\nإِنَّ مَا تُوعَدُونَ لَآتٍ وَمَا أَنتُم بِمُعْجِزِينَ\n(Surely, that which you are promised, will verily, come to pass and you cannot escape.) means, tell them, O Muhammad, that what they have been promised of Resurrection will surely occur,\nوَمَا أَنتُم بِمُعْجِزِينَ\n(and you cannot escape.) from Allah. Rather, He is able to resurrect you even after you become dust and bones. Certainly, Allah is able to do all things and nothing ever escapes His power. Allah said;\nقُلْ يَا قَوْمِ اعْمَلُوا عَلَىٰ مَكَانَتِكُمْ إِنِّي عَامِلٌ فَسَوْفَ تَعْلَمُونَ\n(Say: \"O my people! Work according to your way, surely, I too am working and you will come to know.\")\nThis contains a stern warning and a sure promise, saying; remain on your way, if you think that you are rightly guided, for I will remain on mine. Allah said in another Ayah,\nوَقُل لِّلَّذِينَ لَا يُؤْمِنُونَ اعْمَلُوا عَلَىٰ مَكَانَتِكُمْ إِنَّا عَامِلُونَ - وَانتَظِرُوا إِنَّا مُنتَظِرُونَ\n(And say to those who do not believe: \"Act according to 'Makānatikum', We are acting (in our way). And you wait! We (too) are waiting.\")[11:121-122]. 'Ali bin Abi Talhah reported that Ibn 'Abbas said that,\nعَلَىٰ مَكَانَتِكُمْ\n(according to 'Makānatikum'...) means, your way.\nفَسَوْفَ تَعْلَمُونَ مَن تَكُونُ لَهُ عَاقِبَةُ الدَّارِ إِنَّهُ لَا يُفْلِحُ الظَّالِمُونَ\n(And you will come to know for which of us will be the (happy) end in the Hereafter. Certainly the wrongdoers will not be successful)[6:135],\nYou will come to know if the happy end will be mine (Muhammad's) or yours (the disbelievers). Allah has indeed kept His promise and allowed Muhammad ﷺ to prevail in the land and rise above those who defied him. He conquered Makkah for him and made him triumphant over his people who rejected and showed enmity towards him. The Prophet's rule soon spread over the Arabian Peninsula, Yemen and Bahrain, and all this occurred during his lifetime. After his death, the various lands and provinces were conquered during the time of his successors, may Allah be pleased with them all. Allah also said,\nكَتَبَ اللَّهُ لَأَغْلِبَنَّ أَنَا وَرُسُلِي إِنَّ اللَّهَ قَوِيٌّ عَزِيزٌ\n(Allah has decreed: \"Verily, it is I and My Messengers who shall be the victorious.\" Verily, Allah is All-Powerful, Almighty.)[58:21]\nإِنَّا لَنَنصُرُ رُسُلَنَا وَالَّذِينَ آمَنُوا فِي الْحَيَاةِ الدُّنْيَا وَيَوْمَ يَقُومُ الْأَشْهَادُ - يَوْمَ لَا يَنفَعُ الظَّالِمِينَ مَعْذِرَتُهُمْ وَلَهُمُ اللَّعْنَةُ وَلَهُمْ سُوءُ الدَّارِ\n(Verily, We will indeed make victorious Our Messengers and those who believe in this world's life and on the Day when the witnesses will stand forth. The Day when their excuses will be of no profit to the wrongdoers. Theirs will be the curse, and theirs will be the evil abode.)[40:51-52] and,\nوَلَقَدْ كَتَبْنَا فِي الزَّبُورِ مِن بَعْدِ الذِّكْرِ أَنَّ الْأَرْضَ يَرِثُهَا عِبَادِيَ الصَّالِحُونَ\n(And indeed We have written in the Zabur after the Dhikr that My righteous servants shall inherit the land.)[21:105]","href":"/tafsir/6?verse=133","kind":"tafsir"},{"id":"tafsir:6:136","title":"Ibn Kathir · 6:136","text":"And they assign to Allah a share of the tilth and cattle which He has created, and they say: \"This is for Allah,\" according to their claim, \"and this is for our partners.\" But the share of their \"partners\" reaches not Allah, while the share of Allah reaches their \"partners\"! Evil is the way they judge (136)\n\n## Some Acts of Shirk\n\nAllah chastises and criticizes the idolators who invented innovations, Kufr and Shirk, and called on partners and rivals with Allah among His creation, although He created every thing, all praise is due to Him. This is why Allah said,\nوَجَعَلُوا لِلَّهِ مِمَّا ذَرَأَ\n(And they assign to Allah from that which He has created,)\nمِنَ الْحَرْثِ\n(of the tilth) meaning, fruits and produce,\nوَالْأَنْعَامِ نَصِيبًا\n(and of the cattle a share) meaning a part and a section.\nفَقَالُوا هَٰذَا لِلَّهِ بِزَعْمِهِمْ وَهَٰذَا لِشُرَكَائِنَا\n(and they say: \"This is for Allah,\" according to their claim, \"and this is for our partners.\") Allah said next,\nفَمَا كَانَ لِشُرَكَائِهِمْ فَلَا يَصِلُ إِلَى اللَّهِ وَمَا كَانَ لِلَّهِ فَهُوَ يَصِلُ إِلَىٰ شُرَكَائِهِمْ\n(But the share of their \"partners\" reaches not Allah, while the share of Allah reaches their \"partners\"!)\nAli bin Abi Talhah and Al-'Awfi narrated that Ibn 'Abbas said; \"When they, the enemies of Allah, would cultivate the land or collect produce, they would assign a part of it to Allah and another part to the idol. They would keep the share for the idol, whether land, produce or anything else, and preserve its division to such an extent that they would collect anything that accidentally falls from the share they assigned to Allah and add it to the share of the idol. If the water that they assigned for the idol irrigated something (a section of land, for instance) that they assigned for Allah, they would add whatever this water irrigated to the idol's share! If the land or produce that they assigned for Allah was accidentally mixed with the share that they assigned for the idol, they would say that the idol is poor. Therefore, they would add it to the share they assigned for the idol and would not return it to the share they assigned for Allah. If the water that they assigned for Allah irrigated what they assigned for the idol they would leave it (the produce) for the idol. They also made some of their other property sacred, like the 'Baḥīrah', 'Sā'ibah', 'Waşīlah' and 'Ḥām', assigning them to the idols, claiming that they do so as way of seeking a means of approach to Allah. Allah said,\nوَجَعَلُوا لِلَّهِ مِمَّا ذَرَأَ مِنَ الْحَرْثِ وَالْأَنْعَامِ نَصِيبًا\n(And they assign to Allah a share of the tilth and cattle which He has created...).\"\nSimilar was said by Mujahid, Qatadah, As-Suddi and others. 'Abdur-Rahman bin Zayd bin Aslam commented; \"Every type of slaughter that they would assign for Allah, would never be eaten unless they mentioned the names of their idols when slaughtering it. Yet for what they sacrificed in the names of the idols, they would not mention Allah's Name when slaughtering it.\" He then recited the Ayah (6:136) until he reached,\nسَاءَ مَا يَحْكُمُونَ\n(Evil is the way they judge!)\nThis Ayah means, evil is that which they determined, for they committed error in the division. Certainly, Allah is the Lord, Owner and Creator of all things and His is the dominion. All things are His property and under His supreme control, will and decree. There is no deity worthy of worship, or Lord, except Him.\nAnd even when the polytheists made this evil division, they did not preserve it, but cheated in it. Allah said in other Ayat,\nوَيَجْعَلُونَ لِلَّهِ الْبَنَاتِ سُبْحَانَهُ وَلَهُم مَّا يَشْتَهُونَ\n(And they assign daughters unto Allah – glory be to Him – and unto themselves what they desire.)[16:57], and\nوَجَعَلُوا لَهُ مِنْ عِبَادِهِ جُزْءًا إِنَّ الْإِنسَانَ لَكَفُورٌ مُّبِينٌ\n(Yet, they assign to some of His servants a share with Him. Verily, man is indeed a manifest ingrate!)[43:15], and,\nأَلَكُمُ الذَّكَرُ وَلَهُ الْأُنثَىٰ - تِلْكَ إِذًا قِسْمَةٌ ضِيزَىٰ\n(Is it for you the males and for Him the females? That indeed is a division most unfair!)[53:21-22].","href":"/tafsir/6?verse=136","kind":"tafsir"},{"id":"tafsir:6:137","title":"Ibn Kathir · 6:137","text":"And so to many of the idolators, their \"partners\" have made fair seeming the killing of their children, in order to lead them to their own destruction and cause confusion in their religion. And if Allah had willed, they would not have done so. So leave them alone with their fabrications (137)\n\n## Shaytan Lured the Idolators to Kill Their Children\n\nAllah says, just as the Shayatin lured the idolators to assign a share for Allah from what He created of agriculture and cattle - and a share for the idols, they also made it seem fair for them to kill their children, for fear of poverty, and burying their daughters alive, for fear of dishonor.\nAli bin Abi Talhah reported from Ibn 'Abbas that he commented;\nوَكَذَٰلِكَ زَيَّنَ لِكَثِيرٍ مِّنَ الْمُشْرِكِينَ قَتْلَ أَوْلَادِهِمْ شُرَكَاؤُهُمْ\n(And so to many of the idolators, their \"partners\" have made fair seeming the killing of their children...)\n\"They make killing their children attractive to them.\"\nMujahid said, \"Idolators' partners among the devils ordered them to bury their children for fear of poverty.\" As-Suddi said, \"The devils commanded them to kill their daughters so that they,\nلِيُرْدُوهُمْ\n(lead them to their own destruction), and to,\nوَلِيَلْبِسُوا عَلَيْهِمْ دِينَهُمْ\n(cause confusion in their religion.)\" Allah said,\nوَلَوْ شَاءَ اللَّهُ مَا فَعَلُوهُ\n(And if Allah had willed, they would not have done so.) meaning, all this occurred by Allah's leave, will and decree, but He dislikes these practices, and He has the perfect wisdom in every decree. He is never questioned about what He does, but they all will be questioned.\nفَذَرْهُمْ وَمَا يَفْتَرُونَ\n(So leave them alone with their fabrications.) meaning, avoid and abandon them and what they do, for Allah will judge between you and them.","href":"/tafsir/6?verse=137","kind":"tafsir"},{"id":"tafsir:6:138","title":"Ibn Kathir · 6:138","text":"And according to their claim, they say that such and such cattle and crops are Hijr (forbidden), and none should eat of them except those whom we allow. He will recompense them for what they used to fabricate (138)\n\n## The Idolators Forbade Certain Types of Cattle\n\nAli bin Abi Talhah reported that Ibn 'Abbas said, \"Hijr refers to what they forbade, such as the Wasilah, and the like.\"\nSimilar was said by Mujahid, Ad-Dahhak, As-Suddi, Qatadah, 'Abdur-Rahman bin Zayd bin Aslam and others. 'Awfi said that Ibn 'Abbas said about this Ayah,\nوَقَالُوا مَا فِي بُطُونِ هَٰذِهِ الْأَنْعَامِ خَالِصَةٌ لِّذُكُورِنَا\n(And they say: \"What is in the bellies of such and such cattle is for our males alone...\")\n\"It is about milk, which they prohibited for their females and allowed only their males to drink. When a sheep would give birth to a male sheep, they would slaughter it and feed it to their males, but not to their females. If the newly born lamb was a female, they would not slaughter it, but if it was stillborn, they would share in it (with their females)!
